import React from 'react';
import { FamiChatLogo } from './FamiChatLogo';

interface SplashScreenProps {
  darkMode?: boolean;
}

export const SplashScreen: React.FC<SplashScreenProps> = ({ darkMode = true }) => {
  return (
    <div
      id="famichat-splash-screen"
      className={`fixed inset-0 z-[99999] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 select-none overflow-hidden transition-colors duration-300 ${
        darkMode ? 'bg-slate-950 text-white' : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-900'
      }`}
    >
      {/* Resplandores de ambiente (violeta + coral) */}
      <div className="absolute -top-24 -left-24 w-80 h-80 bg-emerald-500/20 rounded-full blur-[110px] pointer-events-none" />
      <div className="absolute -bottom-24 -right-24 w-80 h-80 bg-teal-500/20 rounded-full blur-[110px] pointer-events-none" />

      <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center animate-splash-fade-in z-10 max-w-xs w-full">
        {/* Icono de app: cuadrado redondeado con gradiente de marca */}
        <div className="relative mb-6">
          <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-[30px] bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-500 to-teal-500 flex items-center justify-center shadow-2xl shadow-emerald-500/40 animate-heartbeat">
            <FamiChatLogo className="w-14 h-14 text-white" />
          </div>
        </div>

        <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight font-sans mb-1">
          Fami<span className="text-teal-500">Chat</span>
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 mb-7">Tu familia, siempre cerca</p>

        <div className="w-36 h-1.5 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden relative">
          <div className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-teal-500 rounded-full animate-indeterminate-bar" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
ARCHIVO: src/components/UpdateModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ReleaseInfo } from '../config/version';
import { DownloadCloud, X, ArrowDownCircle, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';

interface UpdateModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  release: ReleaseInfo | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onUpdateNow: () => void;
}

export const UpdateModal: React.FC<UpdateModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  release,
  onClose,
  onUpdateNow
}) => {
  // Solo interfaz: muestra u oculta la ayuda del conflicto de instalación
  const [showInstallHelp, setShowInstallHelp] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen || !release) return null;

  const formatBodyLines = (text: string) => {
    if (!text) return ['Nuevas mejoras de rendimiento y estabilidad para la familia.'];
    const lines = text
      .split('\n')
      .map(l => l.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);
    return lines.slice(0, 5);
  };

  const bodyLines = formatBodyLines(release.body);
  const versionLabel = release.version.startsWith('v') ? release.version : `v${release.version}`;

  return (
    <div
      id="update-modal-overlay"
      className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-950/70 backdrop-blur-sm z-[10000] flex items-end justify-center select-none"
      style={{ animation: 'fc-fade 200ms ease-out' }}
    >
      <style>{`
        @keyframes fc-fade { from { opacity: 0; } to { opacity: 1; } }
        @keyframes fc-slide-up { from { transform: translateY(100%); } to { transform: translateY(0); } }
        @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
          #update-modal-overlay, #update-modal-card { animation: none !important; }
        }
      `}</style>

      <div
        id="update-modal-card"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="update-modal-title"
        className="w-full max-w-[430px] bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 rounded-t-[24px] shadow-2xl border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 overflow-hidden"
        style={{
          animation: 'fc-slide-up 250ms cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1)',
          paddingBottom: 'calc(max(env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px), var(--sab, 0px)) + 16px)',
        }}
      >
        {/* Barra de arrastre */}
        <div className="pt-2.5 pb-1 flex justify-center">
          <div className="w-10 h-1 rounded-full bg-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-700" />
        </div>

        {/* Encabezado */}
        <div className="px-5 pt-2 pb-3 flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-[12px] bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-600 to-teal-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-md shadow-emerald-700/20">
            <DownloadCloud className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <h3 id="update-modal-title" className="font-bold text-[17px] leading-tight">
              Actualización disponible: {versionLabel}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Nueva versión de FamiChat</p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Cerrar"
            title="Cerrar"
            className="w-11 h-11 -mr-2 rounded-[12px] flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 active:bg-slate-100 dark:active:bg-slate-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="px-5 space-y-3">
          {/* Notas de la versión */}
          <div className="bg-emerald-50/60 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border border-emerald-100 dark:border-slate-700/60 rounded-[16px] p-4 space-y-2">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 block">
              Notas de la versión
            </span>
            <div className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 space-y-1.5 leading-relaxed">
              {bodyLines.map((line, idx) => (
                <p key={idx} className="flex items-start gap-2">
                  <span className="text-emerald-500 font-bold shrink-0">•</span>
                  <span
                    style={{
                      display: '-webkit-box',
                      WebkitLineClamp: 2,
                      WebkitBoxOrient: 'vertical',
                      overflow: 'hidden',
                    }}
                  >
                    {line.replace(/^[-*#]\s*/, '')}
                  </span>
                </p>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Indicación de instalación + ayuda plegable */}
          <div className="bg-teal-50 dark:bg-teal-950/40 px-4 py-3 rounded-[16px] border border-teal-200/50 dark:border-teal-800/40">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300">
              <ArrowDownCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400 shrink-0" />
              <span>Toca Instalar al terminar de descargar.</span>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowInstallHelp(v => !v)}
              aria-expanded={showInstallHelp}
              className="mt-1 ml-7 min-h-[44px] flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <span>¿Problemas al instalar?</span>
              <ChevronDown className={`w-4 h-4 transition-transform duration-150 ${showInstallHelp ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
            </button>

            {showInstallHelp && (
              <p className="ml-7 pb-1 text-[13px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-snug">
                Si Android dice que hay un conflicto, desinstala la versión anterior una sola vez e instala esta.
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Botones */}
          <div className="pt-1 flex gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="flex-1 h-12 px-3 rounded-[12px] border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 active:bg-slate-100 dark:active:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold text-[15px] transition-colors cursor-pointer text-center"
            >
              Más tarde
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onUpdateNow}
              className="flex-[1.4] h-12 px-3 rounded-[12px] bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-600 active:scale-[0.97] text-white font-bold text-[15px] shadow-md shadow-emerald-700/20 transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <DownloadCloud className="w-5 h-5 shrink-0" />
              <span>Actualizar ahora</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

ARCHIVO: src/components/UserSwitcher.tsx
================================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Contact } from '../types';
import { Shield, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';

interface UserSwitcherProps {
  contacts: Contact[];
  currentUser: Contact;
  onUserChange: (user: Contact) => void;
  isSwitcherUnlocked?: boolean;
  onUnlockRequest?: () => void;
  onBackToSelectionRequest?: () => void;
  moreMenu?: React.ReactNode;
}

export const UserSwitcher: React.FC<UserSwitcherProps> = ({
  contacts,
  currentUser,
  onUserChange,
  isSwitcherUnlocked = false,
  onUnlockRequest,
  onBackToSelectionRequest,
  moreMenu,
}) => {
  const isPrivileged = currentUser.role === 'admin' || isSwitcherUnlocked;

  return (
    <div id="user-identity-switcher" className="bg-slate-900 border-b border-teal-500/15 text-white px-3 sm:px-4 py-2 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-stretch md:items-center justify-between gap-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm z-30 shadow-md">
      {/* Left side: Active user identity info */}
      <div className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between md:justify-start gap-2 pr-1 min-w-0">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0">
          <span className="flex h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-450 shrink-0" />
          <div className="text-slate-400 font-bold text-[10.5px] uppercase tracking-wider select-none shrink-0">
            Multiperfil:
          </div>
          <div className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm border border-white/5 ${currentUser.avatarColor} truncate max-w-[140px] xs:max-w-none`}>
            <span>{currentUser.avatarEmoji}</span>
            <span className="truncate">{currentUser.name}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Small desktop indicator label */}
        {currentUser.role === 'admin' && (
          <span className="bg-amber-400/10 text-amber-350 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[8px] font-black tracking-wider uppercase shrink-0 border border-amber-400/10 hidden xs:inline-flex items-center gap-0.5">
            <Shield className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> PAPÁ
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      
      {/* Right side: Dynamic profiles or actions and MoreMenu kebab */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0 max-w-full justify-between md:justify-end flex-wrap sm:flex-nowrap">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 flex-1 sm:flex-initial min-w-0">
          {isPrivileged ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 w-full">
              {/* Back to selection page directly (since already authorized/admin) */}
              <button
                onClick={onBackToSelectionRequest}
                className="bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-750 text-slate-300 hover:text-white text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-700/60 transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0"
                title="Volver a la selección general de perfiles"
              >
                🔄 <span>Selección</span>
              </button>

              <div className="flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-none py-0.5 w-full">
                {contacts.map((c) => {
                  const isSelected = c.id === currentUser.id;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={c.id}
                      id={`switch-to-${c.id}`}
                      onClick={() => onUserChange(c)}
                      className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs transition-all tracking-wide font-medium relative whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'bg-teal-500/25 text-teal-350 border border-teal-500/40 font-bold shadow-inner'
                          : 'text-slate-350 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 border border-transparent'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span>{c.avatarEmoji}</span>
                      <span>{c.name.split(' ')[0]}</span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 w-full">
              {/* Unlock switcher option (requests PIN) */}
              <button
                onClick={onUnlockRequest}
                className="bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-750 text-slate-200 text-[10px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-700/60 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm cursor-pointer flex-1 justify-center whitespace-nowrap"
                title="Desbloquear resto de cuentas de la familia"
              >
                🔒 <span>Habilitar Switcher</span>
              </button>

              {/* Back to Selection options with password challenge safeguard */}
              <button
                onClick={onBackToSelectionRequest}
                className="bg-emerald-650 hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white text-[10px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-emerald-500/10 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm cursor-pointer flex-1 justify-center whitespace-nowrap"
                title="Volver a selección general (solicita PIN de administrador)"
              >
                🔄 <span>Cambiar Perfil</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Integration slot for the Kebab MoreOptions menu */}
        {moreMenu && (
          <div className="shrink-0 border-l border-slate-800 pl-1.5">
            {moreMenu}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
ARCHIVO: src/components/VoiceMessagePlayer.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Play, Pause } from 'lucide-react';

interface VoiceMessagePlayerProps {
  mediaUrl: string;
}

export const VoiceMessagePlayer: React.FC<VoiceMessagePlayerProps> = ({ mediaUrl }) => {
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false);
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(0);
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
  const [playbackRate, setPlaybackRate] = useState<number>(1);
  const audioRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const audio = new Audio(mediaUrl);
    audioRef.current = audio;

    const handleLoadedMetadata = () => {
      if (audio.duration && audio.duration !== Infinity && !isNaN(audio.duration)) {
        setDuration(audio.duration);
      } else if (audio.duration === Infinity) {
        audio.currentTime = 1e101;
        const fixDuration = () => {
          audio.removeEventListener('timeupdate', fixDuration);
          audio.currentTime = 0;
          if (audio.duration && audio.duration !== Infinity && !isNaN(audio.duration)) {
            setDuration(audio.duration);
          }
        };
        audio.addEventListener('timeupdate', fixDuration);
      }
    };

    const handleTimeUpdate = () => {
      setCurrentTime(audio.currentTime);
    };

    const handleEnded = () => {
      setIsPlaying(false);
      setCurrentTime(0);
    };

    audio.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', handleLoadedMetadata);
    audio.addEventListener('timeupdate', handleTimeUpdate);
    audio.addEventListener('ended', handleEnded);

    // Try loading audio metadata
    audio.load();

    // Check duration periodically if it's Infinity initially (common for dynamic Base64 chunks)
    const checkDurationInterval = setInterval(() => {
      if (audio.duration && audio.duration !== Infinity && duration === 0) {
        setDuration(audio.duration);
        clearInterval(checkDurationInterval);
      }
    }, 400);

    return () => {
      audio.pause();
      audio.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata', handleLoadedMetadata);
      audio.removeEventListener('timeupdate', handleTimeUpdate);
      audio.removeEventListener('ended', handleEnded);
      clearInterval(checkDurationInterval);
    };
  }, [mediaUrl]);

  // Handle speed changes cleanly
  useEffect(() => {
    if (audioRef.current) {
      audioRef.current.playbackRate = playbackRate;
    }
  }, [playbackRate]);

  const togglePlay = () => {
    if (!audioRef.current) return;

    if (isPlaying) {
      audioRef.current.pause();
      setIsPlaying(false);
    } else {
      audioRef.current.play()
        .then(() => {
          setIsPlaying(true);
        })
        .catch(err => {
          console.warn("Playback blocked by browser audio policy or incomplete stream:", err);
        });
    }
  };

  const handleSliderChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (!audioRef.current) return;
    const value = parseFloat(e.target.value);
    audioRef.current.currentTime = value;
    setCurrentTime(value);
  };

  const toggleSpeed = () => {
    setPlaybackRate(prev => {
      if (prev === 1) return 1.5;
      if (prev === 1.5) return 2;
      return 1;
    });
  };

  const formatTime = (secs: number) => {
    if (isNaN(secs) || secs === Infinity) return '0:00';
    const mins = Math.floor(secs / 60);
    const remaining = Math.floor(secs % 60);
    return `${mins}:${remaining < 10 ? '0' : ''}${remaining}`;
  };

  // Generate visual representations for progress tracking
  const percentage = duration > 0 ? (currentTime / duration) * 100 : 0;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 w-full bg-black/5 dark:bg-black/10 p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-black/5 select-none my-1">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        {/* Play/Pause Button */}
        <button
          onClick={togglePlay}
          type="button"
          className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-emerald-550 hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 cursor-pointer shadow transition-all active:scale-95"
          title={isPlaying ? "Pausar" : "Reproducir nota de voz"}
        >
          {isPlaying ? (
            <Pause className="w-4 h-4 fill-current text-white" />
          ) : (
            <Play className="w-4 h-4 fill-current text-white ml-0.5" />
          )}
        </button>

        {/* Custom Progress Scrubber Channel */}
        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center min-w-0">
          <div className="relative flex items-center h-4 w-full">
            {/* Native slider configured on top of standard styling */}
            <input
              type="range"
              min={0}
              max={duration || 100}
              step={0.05}
              value={currentTime}
              onChange={handleSliderChange}
              className="w-full accent-emerald-600 h-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-100/60 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 cursor-pointer outline-none appearance-none"
              style={{
                background: `linear-gradient(to right, #059669 0%, #059669 ${percentage}%, #cbd5e1 ${percentage}%, #cbd5e1 100%)`
              }}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono mt-0.5">
            <span>{formatTime(currentTime)}</span>
            <span>{duration > 0 ? formatTime(duration) : '0:15'}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Fast Play Rate Button */}
        <button
          onClick={toggleSpeed}
          type="button"
          className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-250 text-slate-700 px-2 py-1 rounded-md transition-all cursor-pointer font-sans shrink-0 min-w-8 text-center"
          title="Velocidad de reproducción"
        >
          {playbackRate}x
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
ARCHIVO: src/components/WelcomeGateway.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Contact } from '../types';
import { ShieldCheck, Lock, ArrowLeft, AlertCircle, Delete } from 'lucide-react';

interface WelcomeGatewayProps {
  contacts: Contact[];
  onSelectUser: (user: Contact) => void;
}

// Letras decorativas bajo cada número (solo diseño, el PIN sigue siendo numérico)
const KEY_LETTERS: Record<string, string> = {
  '1': '', '2': 'ABC', '3': 'DEF', '4': 'GHI', '5': 'JKL',
  '6': 'MNO', '7': 'PQRS', '8': 'TUV', '9': 'WXYZ', '0': '',
};

const KEY_HEIGHT = 'clamp(52px, 8.2dvh, 76px)';

export const WelcomeGateway: React.FC<WelcomeGatewayProps> = ({ contacts, onSelectUser }) => {
  const [selectedCandidate, setSelectedCandidate] = useState<Contact | null>(null);
  const [pinCode, setPinCode] = useState<string>('');
  const [pinError, setPinError] = useState<string>('');
  const [isSuccess, setIsSuccess] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [shake, setShake] = useState<boolean>(false); // solo animación visual

  const getExpectedPin = () => {
    return localStorage.getItem('famichat_admin_pin') || '1122';
  };

  const handleSelect = (user: Contact) => {
    if (user.role === 'admin') {
      setSelectedCandidate(user);
      setPinCode('');
      setPinError('');
      setIsSuccess(false);
    } else {
      // Normal family members enter directly with absolute privacy active
      onSelectUser(user);
    }
  };

  // Retroalimentación visual/táctil al fallar (no afecta la lógica)
  const triggerErrorFeedback = () => {
    setShake(true);
    setTimeout(() => setShake(false), 350);
    try {
      if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && 'vibrate' in navigator) {
        navigator.vibrate(120);
      }
    } catch {
      /* si el dispositivo no vibra, se ignora */
    }
  };

  const handleKeypadPress = (num: string) => {
    setPinError('');
    const expected = getExpectedPin();
    if (pinCode.length >= expected.length) return;
    const nextPin = pinCode + num;
    setPinCode(nextPin);

    if (nextPin === expected) {
      setIsSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (selectedCandidate) {
          onSelectUser(selectedCandidate);
        }
      }, 700);
    } else if (nextPin.length === expected.length) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        setPinError('Clave de administrador incorrecta. Acceso denegado.');
        setPinCode('');
        triggerErrorFeedback();
      }, 150);
    }
  };

  const handleBackspace = () => {
    setPinCode(prev => prev.slice(0, -1));
    setPinError('');
  };

  const KEYPAD_NUMBERS = ['1', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6', '7', '8', '9'];

  // Tecla numérica: número arriba + letras pequeñas debajo (línea siempre reservada)
  const renderNumberKey = (num: string) => (
    <button
      key={num}
      type="button"
      disabled={isSuccess}
      onClick={() => handleKeypadPress(num)}
      aria-label={`Número ${num}`}
      style={{ height: KEY_HEIGHT }}
      className="w-full rounded-[18px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-700/60 text-white transition-all duration-100 active:scale-95 active:bg-emerald-400/15 active:border-emerald-400/50 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-emerald-400 disabled:opacity-60 cursor-pointer select-none"
    >
      <span className="text-[28px] sm:text-[30px] font-semibold leading-none tabular-nums">{num}</span>
      <span className="mt-1 h-[11px] text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] leading-none tracking-[0.2em] text-slate-400">
        {KEY_LETTERS[num]}
      </span>
    </button>
  );

  if (selectedCandidate) {
    const expectedLength = getExpectedPin().length;

    return (
      <div
        id="welcome-identity-gateway"
        className="fixed inset-0 h-[100dvh] w-full bg-slate-900 bg-gradient-to-tr from-slate-950 via-teal-950 to-emerald-950 text-white z-[99999] flex flex-col overflow-hidden select-none"
        style={{
          paddingTop: 'calc(max(env(safe-area-inset-top, 0px), var(--sat, 0px)) + 10px)',
          paddingBottom: 'calc(max(env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px), var(--sab, 0px)) + 12px)',
        }}
      >
        <style>{`
          @keyframes fc-shake {
            0%, 100% { transform: translateX(0); }
            20% { transform: translateX(-8px); }
            40% { transform: translateX(8px); }
            60% { transform: translateX(-6px); }
            80% { transform: translateX(6px); }
          }
          .fc-shake { animation: fc-shake 300ms ease-in-out; }
          @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
            .fc-shake { animation: none; }
          }
        `}</style>

        {/* Tarjeta contenedora con borde */}
        <div className="w-full max-w-[430px] flex-1 min-h-0 mx-auto px-3">
          <div className="w-full h-full rounded-[28px] border border-slate-700/60 bg-slate-950/70 backdrop-blur px-4 pt-3 pb-2 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">

            {/* BLOQUE 1 (ARRIBA): Título, avatar y puntos del PIN */}
            <div className="w-full shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center">
              <span className="text-xs font-black tracking-[0.2em] uppercase text-emerald-300/90 font-sans">
                FamiChat
              </span>

              <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center mt-2">
                <div
                  className={`flex items-center justify-center rounded-[20px] border border-emerald-400/40 shadow-md text-3xl sm:text-4xl ${selectedCandidate.avatarColor}`}
                  style={{ width: 'clamp(48px, 7.5dvh, 68px)', height: 'clamp(48px, 7.5dvh, 68px)' }}
                >
                  {selectedCandidate.avatarEmoji}
                </div>

                <h3 className="mt-2 font-bold text-white text-[19px] sm:text-[22px] leading-tight">
                  {selectedCandidate.name}
                </h3>

                <p className="mt-0.5 text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] text-amber-400 font-semibold tracking-[0.16em] uppercase flex items-center gap-1.5 justify-center">
                  <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Acceso Protegido de Administrador</span>
                </p>

                <p className="mt-1 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300">Ingresa tu PIN</p>
              </div>

              {/* Puntos del PIN */}
              <div className={`flex justify-center gap-4 sm:gap-5 mt-2.5 ${shake ? 'fc-shake' : ''}`}>
                {Array.from({ length: expectedLength }).map((_, idx) => (
                  <div
                    key={idx}
                    className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 sm:w-4 sm:h-4 rounded-full border-2 transition-colors duration-100 ${
                      idx < pinCode.length
                        ? 'bg-emerald-400 border-emerald-400 shadow-[0_0_10px_#9486fb]'
                        : 'border-slate-600 bg-slate-800'
                    }`}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* Mensajes (altura reservada para que el teclado no salte) */}
              <div className="w-full min-h-[34px] mt-1.5 flex items-center justify-center" aria-live="polite">
                {pinError && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-rose-300 font-semibold bg-rose-950/60 border border-rose-800 rounded-[12px] px-3 py-1.5 justify-center text-center">
                    <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                    <span>{pinError}</span>
                  </div>
                )}
                {isSuccess && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-emerald-300 font-bold bg-emerald-950/60 border border-emerald-800 rounded-[12px] px-3 py-1.5 justify-center text-center">
                    <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
                    <span>Llave Correcta. Iniciando...</span>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* BLOQUE 2 (MEDIO): Espacio flexible */}
            <div className="flex-1 min-h-1" />

            {/* BLOQUE 3 (ABAJO): Teclado 3x4 */}
            <div className="w-full shrink-0">
              <div
                className="grid w-full"
                style={{ gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3, 1fr)', gap: '9px' }}
              >
                {KEYPAD_NUMBERS.map(renderNumberKey)}

                {/* Fila 4: [vacío]  0  [borrar] */}
                <div style={{ height: KEY_HEIGHT }} aria-hidden="true" />
                {renderNumberKey('0')}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={isSuccess}
                  onClick={handleBackspace}
                  aria-label="Borrar"
                  title="Borrar"
                  style={{ height: KEY_HEIGHT }}
                  className="w-full rounded-[18px] flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/40 border border-slate-700/40 text-slate-200 transition-all duration-100 active:scale-95 active:bg-emerald-400/15 active:border-emerald-400/50 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-emerald-400 disabled:opacity-60 cursor-pointer select-none"
                >
                  <Delete className="w-7 h-7" />
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Línea divisoria y botón Volver como enlace de texto */}
              <div className="mt-2 border-t border-slate-700/50">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedCandidate(null)}
                  className="w-full h-11 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-300 active:text-white transition-colors duration-100 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-emerald-400 rounded-[12px]"
                >
                  <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>Volver</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div 
      id="welcome-identity-gateway" 
      className="fixed inset-0 h-[100dvh] w-full bg-slate-900 bg-gradient-to-tr from-slate-950 via-teal-950 to-emerald-950 text-white z-[99999] flex flex-col justify-between items-center overflow-y-auto px-4 pt-[calc(var(--sat,0px)+0.75rem)] md:pt-6 pb-[calc(var(--sab,0px)+0.5rem)] md:pb-6 select-none"
    >
      {/* Top ambient brand head */}
      <div className="text-center shrink-0 animate-fade-in">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 px-3 py-0.5 rounded-full mb-1 text-[9.5px] font-bold tracking-[0.16em] text-emerald-300 uppercase">
          🛡️ Red Familiar Privada
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black tracking-tight bg-clip-text text-transparent bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-150 via-teal-100 to-emerald-300 font-sans leading-tight">
          FamiChat
        </h1>
        <p className="text-[10.5px] text-teal-300/80 font-serif italic">by Martinez Alejandro</p>
      </div>

      {/* Center Dynamic Card Container */}
      <div className="w-full max-w-[340px] sm:max-w-sm mx-auto my-1.5 flex flex-col items-center shrink-0">
        <div className="w-full bg-white/5 backdrop-blur-md border border-white/10 rounded-3xl p-4 sm:p-6 shadow-[0_24px_64px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5)] space-y-3.5 animate-scale-up">
          <div className="text-center space-y-1">
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-extrabold text-white text-center">¿Quién está ingresando hoy?</h2>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-white/60 leading-snug max-w-[250px] mx-auto text-center">
              Selecciona tu perfil para cargar tus mensajes privados y notas familiares del teléfono.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Avatars Listing Grid */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
            {contacts.map((c) => (
              <button
                key={c.id}
                onClick={() => handleSelect(c)}
                className="group relative flex flex-col items-center p-3 bg-white/[0.03] hover:bg-white/[0.12] border border-white/5 hover:border-emerald-500/30 rounded-2xl transition-all cursor-pointer shadow-sm text-center active:scale-95 focus:outline-none"
              >
                <div className={`w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-2xl shadow-lg border border-white/10 group-hover:scale-105 duration-200 transition-all ${c.avatarColor}`}>
                  {c.avatarEmoji}
                </div>
                <span className="font-extrabold text-[11.5px] text-white/95 mt-2 block truncate max-w-full">
                  {c.name}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[9px] text-teal-300/70 font-medium block truncate max-w-full italic">
                  {c.relationship} {c.role === 'admin' && '★'}
                </span>

                {c.role === 'admin' && (
                  <span className="absolute top-2 right-2 p-1 bg-amber-500/15 rounded text-[8px] font-black text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/25">
                    PIN
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Low-profile secure credits legend at the bottom */}
      <div className="text-center text-[9.5px] text-white/35 pb-0.5 font-medium select-none shrink-0">
        Encriptación local activa • FamiChat Martínez r2.7
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
ARCHIVO: src/config/version.ts
================================================================================
import { Capacitor, CapacitorHttp } from '@capacitor/core';
import { App as CapApp } from '@capacitor/app';

// ============================================================================
// CONFIGURACIÓN CENTRALIZADA DE REPOSITORIO DE GITHUB Y DETECCIÓN DE VERSIÓN
// ============================================================================

export const GITHUB_OWNER = "cuentaderecuperacion7525-hub";
export const GITHUB_REPO = "AyalaApp";

// Versión de respaldo únicamente cuando se ejecuta en navegador web fuera del APK nativo
const WEB_FALLBACK_VERSION = "1.0.0";

export interface ReleaseInfo {
  version: string;      // Siempre con prefijo "v", ej. "v1.0.15"
  cleanVersion: string; // Sin prefijo "v", ej. "1.0.15"
  body: string;
  downloadUrl: string;
  releaseUrl: string;
}

/**
 * Obtiene la versión real instalada de la aplicación nativa mediante @capacitor/app.
 */
export async function getInstalledAppVersion(): Promise<string> {
  try {
    if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
      const info = await CapApp.getInfo();
      if (info && info.version) {
        return info.version.trim().replace(/^v/i, '');
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('No se pudo leer App.getInfo(), usando versión de respaldo:', e);
  }
  return WEB_FALLBACK_VERSION;
}

/**
 * Quita la "v" del tag_name y compara con la versión instalada numéricamente,
 * por partes (ej. 1.2.41 > 1.2.9).
 * Devuelve:
 *   1 si remoteTag > currentVersion (hay actualización)
 *   0 si son iguales
 *  -1 si remoteTag < currentVersion
 */
export function compareVersions(remoteTag: string, currentVersion: string): number {
  const sanitize = (v: string) => v.trim().replace(/^v/i, '');
  const p1 = sanitize(remoteTag).split('.').map(n => parseInt(n, 10) || 0);
  const p2 = sanitize(currentVersion).split('.').map(n => parseInt(n, 10) || 0);
  const maxLen = Math.max(p1.length, p2.length);

  for (let i = 0; i < maxLen; i++) {
    const num1 = p1[i] ?? 0;
    const num2 = p2[i] ?? 0;
    if (num1 > num2) return 1;
    if (num1 < num2) return -1;
  }
  return 0;
}

/**
 * Consulta la última release publicada en GitHub usando CapacitorHttp nativo
 * para evitar bloqueos CORS del WebView. No envía datos de usuarios ni mensajes.
 */
export async function fetchLatestRelease(installedVersionOverride?: string): Promise<{
  success: boolean;
  hasUpdate: boolean;
  installedVersion: string;
  release?: ReleaseInfo;
  errorMessage?: string;
}> {
  const installedVersion = installedVersionOverride || (await getInstalledAppVersion());

  if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && !navigator.onLine) {
    return {
      success: false,
      hasUpdate: false,
      installedVersion,
      errorMessage: 'Sin internet'
    };
  }

  try {
    const url = `https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_OWNER}/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/latest`;
    const response = await CapacitorHttp.get({
      url,
      headers: {
        'Accept': 'application/vnd.github.v3+json',
        'User-Agent': 'FamiChat-App'
      }
    });

    if (response.status === 404) {
      return {
        success: false,
        hasUpdate: false,
        installedVersion,
        errorMessage: 'No se encontró ninguna release'
      };
    }

    if (response.status < 200 || response.status >= 300) {
      return {
        success: false,
        hasUpdate: false,
        installedVersion,
        errorMessage: `Error al consultar GitHub (código ${response.status})`
      };
    }

    const data = typeof response.data === 'string' ? JSON.parse(response.data) : response.data;
    const rawTag = (data?.tag_name || '').trim();

    if (!rawTag) {
      return {
        success: false,
        hasUpdate: false,
        installedVersion,
        errorMessage: 'No se encontró ninguna release'
      };
    }

    const cleanTag = rawTag.replace(/^v/i, '');
    const displayTag = `v${cleanTag}`;
    const isNewer = compareVersions(cleanTag, installedVersion) > 0;

    // Buscar activo .apk en la release
    let apkDownloadUrl = '';
    if (Array.isArray(data.assets)) {
      const apkAsset = data.assets.find(
        (asset: any) => typeof asset?.name === 'string' && asset.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.apk')
      );
      if (apkAsset?.browser_download_url) {
        apkDownloadUrl = apkAsset.browser_download_url;
      }
    }

    const fallbackReleaseUrl = data.html_url || `https://github.com/${GITHUB_OWNER}/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/latest`;

    return {
      success: true,
      hasUpdate: isNewer,
      installedVersion,
      release: {
        version: displayTag,
        cleanVersion: cleanTag,
        body: data.body || 'Nuevas mejoras y optimizaciones para la familia.',
        downloadUrl: apkDownloadUrl || fallbackReleaseUrl,
        releaseUrl: fallbackReleaseUrl
      }
    };
  } catch (err) {
    const isOffline = typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && !navigator.onLine;
    return {
      success: false,
      hasUpdate: false,
      installedVersion,
      errorMessage: isOffline ? 'Sin internet' : 'No se pudo comprobar. Revisa tu conexión.'
    };
  }
}

import {StrictMode} from 'react';
import {createRoot} from 'react-dom/client';
import {Capacitor} from '@capacitor/core';
import App from './App.tsx';
import '@fontsource-variable/plus-jakarta-sans';
import './index.css';

// In native Android APK context, route relative API requests to backend host
if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
  const originalFetch = window.fetch;
  const REMOTE_BACKEND = 'https://ais-pre-x5o23dw5dokdisr4elvbbw-542088184903.us-west2.run.app';

  window.fetch = async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    if (typeof input === 'string' && input.startsWith('/api')) {
      const serverUrl = localStorage.getItem('famichat_server_url') || REMOTE_BACKEND;
      input = `${serverUrl.replace(/\/$/, '')}${input}`;
    }
    return originalFetch(input, init);
  };
}

// Register Service Worker for true standalone PWA experience without address bar
if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
  const registerSW = () => {
    navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js')
      .then(reg => console.log('Service Worker registered successfully!', reg.scope))
      .catch(err => console.error('Service Worker registration failed:', err));
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'complete' || document.readyState === 'interactive') {
    registerSW();
  } else {
    window.addEventListener('load', registerSW);
  }
}

createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <App />
  </StrictMode>,
);

export type UserRole = 'admin' | 'member';

export interface Contact {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  avatarEmoji: string;
  avatarColor: string; // Tailwind background color class like "bg-emerald-500"
  relationship: string;
  role: UserRole;
  isOnline: boolean;
  lastActive: string; // e.g., "En línea", "Últ. vez hoy a las 12:30", "Últ. vez hace 10 min"
  lastActiveTimestamp?: number;
  phone?: string;
}

export interface Message {
  id: string;
  senderId: string;
  senderName: string;
  receiverId: string; // Can be a contact ID or 'family-group'
  text: string;
  timestamp: Date | string;
  status: 'sent' | 'delivered' | 'read';
  reactions?: string[];
  messageType?: 'text' | 'image' | 'audio';
  mediaUrl?: string; // Contains Base64 data Uri for images/recorded voice audios
  isStarred?: boolean;
  replyToId?: string;
  replyToText?: string;
  replyToSenderName?: string;
}

export interface DirectChat {
  contactId: string;
  messages: Message[];
  unreadCount: number;
  isTyping?: boolean;
}

export interface GroupChat {
  id: 'family-group';
  name: string;
  unreadCount: number;
  messages: Message[];
  isTyping?: { [key: string]: boolean }; // contactId -> typing status
}

export interface LocalNotification {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  avatarEmoji: string;
  avatarColor: string;
  timestamp: string;
}


================================================================================
ARCHIVO: src/utils/api.ts
================================================================================
import { Capacitor, CapacitorHttp } from '@capacitor/core';

/**
 * URL del servidor compartido en la nube para sincronización entre dispositivos (.apk y web).
 */
export const DEFAULT_REMOTE_SERVER_URL = 'https://ais-pre-x5o23dw5dokdisr4elvbbw-542088184903.us-west2.run.app';

export function getApiBaseUrl(): string {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return '';

  const customUrl = localStorage.getItem('famichat_server_url');
  if (customUrl && customUrl.trim()) {
    return customUrl.trim().replace(/\/+$/, '');
  }

  const isNative = Capacitor.isNativePlatform();
  const isLocalhostWebView =
    (window.location.hostname === 'localhost' || window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1') &&
    window.location.port !== '3000';

  if (isNative || isLocalhostWebView) {
    return DEFAULT_REMOTE_SERVER_URL;
  }

  return '';
}

/**
 * Cliente HTTP universal para FamiChat que funciona tanto en navegador web
 * como dentro del APK nativo de Android (usando CapacitorHttp para evitar bloqueos CORS del WebView).
 */
export async function apiFetch(path: string, options: RequestInit = {}): Promise<Response> {
  const baseUrl = getApiBaseUrl();
  const normalizedPath = path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`;
  const fullUrl = `${baseUrl}${normalizedPath}`;

  if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
    try {
      const method = (options.method || 'GET').toUpperCase();
      const headers: Record<string, string> = {
        Accept: 'application/json',
        ...(options.headers as Record<string, string> | undefined),
      };

      let data: any = undefined;
      if (options.body && typeof options.body === 'string') {
        try {
          data = JSON.parse(options.body);
        } catch {
          data = options.body;
        }
      }

      const nativeRes = await CapacitorHttp.request({
        url: fullUrl,
        method,
        headers,
        data,
        connectTimeout: 8000,
        readTimeout: 8000,
      });

      const responseBody =
        typeof nativeRes.data === 'string'
          ? nativeRes.data
          : JSON.stringify(nativeRes.data ?? {});

      return new Response(responseBody, {
        status: nativeRes.status || 200,
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    } catch (nativeErr) {
      // Fallback to standard fetch if CapacitorHttp fails
      return fetch(fullUrl, options);
    }
  }

  return fetch(fullUrl, options);
}

ARCHIVO: src/utils/audio.ts
================================================================================
// Web Audio API Synthesized Sounds
// Works reliably in almost all browsers without external asset dependencies.

let audioCtx: AudioContext | null = null;

function getAudioContext(): AudioContext {
  if (!audioCtx) {
    audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext)();
  }
  return audioCtx;
}

/**
 * Play a high-quality soft bubble pop when sending a message
 */
export function playSendSound() {
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();
    if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {
      ctx.resume();
    }
    
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gainNode = ctx.createGain();
    
    osc.type = 'sine';
    // Small upward pop frequency
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(450, ctx.currentTime);
    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(700, ctx.currentTime + 0.08);
    
    gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, ctx.currentTime);
    gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.08);
    
    osc.connect(gainNode);
    gainNode.connect(ctx.destination);
    
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.08);
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('Audio play block or unsupported:', e);
  }
}

/**
 * Play a beautiful, gentle double-tone electronic bubble alert for incoming notifications
 */
export function playNotificationSound() {
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();
    if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {
      ctx.resume();
    }
    
    const playTone = (freq: number, startDelay: number, duration: number) => {
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gainNode = ctx.createGain();
      
      osc.type = 'triangle';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, ctx.currentTime + startDelay);
      
      gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0, ctx.currentTime + startDelay);
      gainNode.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.08, ctx.currentTime + startDelay + 0.01);
      gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + startDelay + duration);
      
      osc.connect(gainNode);
      gainNode.connect(ctx.destination);
      
      osc.start(ctx.currentTime + startDelay);
      osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + startDelay + duration);
    };
    
    // Modern double chord sweep
    playTone(660, 0, 0.12); // Tone 1
    playTone(880, 0.08, 0.15); // Tone 2
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('Audio play block or unsupported:', e);
  }
}


================================================================================
ARCHIVO: src/utils/date.ts
================================================================================
import { Contact } from '../types';

/**
 * Formats a real timestamp value into an exact connection string (e.g. "Últ. vez hoy a las 18:15")
 * inside the client's local timezone. Never invents fake timestamps.
 */
export function formatLastActive(contact?: Contact | null): string {
  if (!contact) return 'Sin conexión registrada';

  if (contact.isOnline) {
    return 'En línea 🟢';
  }

  const timestamp = contact.lastActiveTimestamp;
  if (!timestamp || typeof timestamp !== 'number' || timestamp <= 0) {
    return 'Sin conexión registrada';
  }

  // Guard against corrupted future timestamps
  const nowMs = Date.now();
  if (timestamp > nowMs + 5 * 60 * 1000) {
    return 'Sin conexión registrada';
  }

  const date = new Date(timestamp);
  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return 'Sin conexión registrada';
  }

  const now = new Date();

  // Compare calendar days in local timezone
  const today = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
  const yesterday = new Date(today);
  yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);

  const targetDate = new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate());

  const timeStr = date.toLocaleTimeString([], {
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
    hour12: true,
  });

  if (targetDate.getTime() === today.getTime()) {
    return `Últ. vez hoy a las ${timeStr}`;
  } else if (targetDate.getTime() === yesterday.getTime()) {
    return `Últ. vez ayer a las ${timeStr}`;
  } else {
    const dateStr = date.toLocaleDateString([], {
      day: '2-digit',
      month: '2-digit',
      year: 'numeric',
    });
    return `Últ. vez el ${dateStr} a las ${timeStr}`;
  }
}

/**
 * Formats the timestamp for the chat list in the sidebar.
 * Outputs hour/minute if today, "Ayer" if yesterday, or the date (DD/MM) if older.
 */
export function formatLastMessageTime(timestamp: Date | string | number | undefined): string {
  if (!timestamp) return '';
  const date = new Date(timestamp);
  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) return '';

  const now = new Date();
  const today = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
  const yesterday = new Date(today);
  yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);

  const targetDate = new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate());

  if (targetDate.getTime() === today.getTime()) {
    return date.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: true });
  } else if (targetDate.getTime() === yesterday.getTime()) {
    return 'Ayer';
  } else {
    return date.toLocaleDateString([], { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit' });
  }
}

/**
 * Helper to display clean text banners for days in active chats.
 */
export function getFormattedDateDivider(timestamp: Date | string): string {
  const date = new Date(timestamp);
  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) return '';

  const now = new Date();
  const today = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
  const yesterday = new Date(today);
  yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);

  const targetDate = new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate());

  if (targetDate.getTime() === today.getTime()) {
    return 'Hoy';
  } else if (targetDate.getTime() === yesterday.getTime()) {
    return 'Ayer';
  } else {
    return date.toLocaleDateString('es-ES', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
  }
}
